[31m+ // Exported only as a pure regression-test seam; it does not participate in Fast Refresh state.[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line react-refresh/only-export-components[39m
[31m+ export function buildTieBreakPitch(relationship: number, playerId: string, week: number): string {[39m
[31m+   const alternate =[39m
[31m+     Math.abs(`${playerId}:${week}`.split('').reduce((sum, char) => sum + char.charCodeAt(0), 0)) %[39m
[31m+       2 ===[39m
[31m+     0[39m
[31m+   if (relationship >= 45)[39m
[31m+     return alternate[39m
[31m+       ? 'We have protected each other before. Keep me, and I will return it.'[39m
[31m+       : 'Our relationship is real. Do not let one tied vote end it.'[39m
[31m+   if (relationship >= 15)[39m
[31m+     return alternate[39m
[31m+       ? 'I can still be a number for you after tonight. Give me that chance.'[39m
[31m+       : 'Keep me and you keep an option in this house?not another enemy.'[39m
[31m+   if (relationship <= -20)[39m
[31m+     return alternate[39m
[31m+       ? 'We are not close, but eliminating me only finishes someone else?s move.'[39m
[31m+       : 'You do not have to trust me to see I am useful as a shield.'[39m
[31m+   return alternate[39m
[31m+     ? 'Give me one more day and judge me by what I do with it.'[39m
[31m+     : 'This decision is yours. I am asking you not to make me the easy answer.'[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ function buildAiOnlyChallengeRawResults(challenge: PendingChallenge) {[39m
[31m+   return challenge.participants.map((id) => ({[39m
[31m+     playerId: id,[39m
[31m+     rawValue: challenge.aiScores[id] ?? 0,[39m
[31m+     ...(challenge.aiTiebreakers?.[id] != null ? { tiebreaker: challenge.aiTiebreakers[id] } : {}),[39m
[31m+   }))[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+   function playerToHouseguest(p: Player) {[39m
[31m+     const isEvicted = p.status === 'evicted' || p.status === 'jury'[39m
[31m+     const parts: string[] = [][39m
[31m+     const povProtectedIds = new Set(game.povProtectedIds ?? [])[39m
[31m+     if (game.lohId === p.id) parts.push('loh')[39m
[31m+     if (game.posWinnerId === p.id) parts.push('pos')[39m
[31m+     if (povProtectedIds.has(p.id)) parts.push('veto_safe')[39m
[31m+     // Suppress permanent nomination badge while the nomination animation is[39m
[31m+     // playing — otherwise AI-LOH nominees (already in game.nomineeIds) would[39m
[31m+     // show the permanent ❓ badge before the animated badge lands.[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingNominee = showNomAnim && nomAnimPlayers.some((n) => n.id === p.id)[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingSaveTarget = pendingSaveCeremony?.savedId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const isPublicSaveWinner = pendingPublicSaveResult?.savedId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingReplacementNominee = activeReplacementAnimationTargetId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingAwardWinner =[39m
[31m+       pendingWinnerCeremony?.winnerId === p.id || (showAdvanceHohCeremony && game.lohId === p.id)[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       Array.isArray(game.nomineeIds) &&[39m
[31m+       game.nomineeIds.includes(p.id) &&[39m
[31m+       !isAnimatingNominee &&[39m
[31m+       !isAnimatingSaveTarget &&[39m
[31m+       !isPublicSaveWinner &&[39m
[31m+       !isAnimatingReplacementNominee[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       parts.push('nominated')[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (p.status === 'jury') parts.push('jury')[39m
[31m+     // When suppressing the nominated badge, also guard the p.status fallback so[39m
[31m+     // that players whose p.status is already 'nominated' (AI-committed nominees)[39m
[31m+     // don't have that status leak through when parts is empty.[39m
[31m+     const suppressFallbackStatus =[39m
[31m+       isAnimatingNominee ||[39m
[31m+       isAnimatingSaveTarget ||[39m
[31m+       isPublicSaveWinner ||[39m
[31m+       isAnimatingReplacementNominee[39m
[31m+     const statuses =[39m
[31m+       parts.length > 0[39m
[31m+         ? parts.join('+')[39m
[31m+         : suppressFallbackStatus[39m
[31m+           ? 'active'[39m
[31m+           : (p.status ?? 'active')[39m
[31m+     const isReturning = battleBackReturnId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const nominationCeremonyState: 'loh' | 'danger' | 'locked' | undefined =[39m
[31m+       !isEvicted && showNominationDangerSignals[39m
[31m+         ? game.lohId === p.id[39m
[31m+           ? 'loh'[39m
[31m+           : nominationDangerLockedId === p.id[39m
[31m+             ? 'locked'[39m
[31m+             : 'danger'[39m
[31m+         : undefined[39m
[31m+     return {[39m
[31m+       id: p.id,[39m
[31m+       name: p.name,[39m
[31m+       avatarUrl: getProfilePhotoAvatarId(p.avatar) ? p.avatar : resolveAvatar(p),[39m
[31m+       statuses,[39m
[31m+       finalRank: (p.finalRank ?? null) as 1 | 2 | 3 | null,[39m
[31m+       isEvicted,[39m
[31m+       isYou: p.isUser,[39m
[31m+       // The landing badge is the only award badge visible during a winner[39m
[31m+       // ceremony. This also covers the outgoing-LOH path, where the reducer has[39m
[31m+       // already committed the winner before the overlay starts.[39m
[31m+       showPermanentBadge: !isAnimatingNominee && !isAnimatingAwardWinner,[39m
[31m+       nominationCeremonyState,[39m
[31m+       layoutId: `avatar-tile-${p.id}`,[39m
[31m+       isEvicting:[39m
[31m+         (showEvictionSplash && pendingEvictionPlayer?.id === p.id) ||[39m
[31m+         game.evictionOverlayPlayerId === p.id ||[39m
[31m+         isReturning,[39m
[31m+       onClick: () => handleAvatarSelect(p),[39m
[31m+       onHoldPreviewStart: () => setPreviewPlayer(p),[39m
[31m+       onHoldPreviewEnd: () =>[39m
[31m+         setPreviewPlayer((current) => (current?.id === p.id ? null : current)),[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m

[31m+   // Shown when a LOH or POS competition is in progress and the human player[39m
[31m+   // is a participant. The Continue button is hidden while the overlay is active.[39m
[31m+   const pendingMinigame = game.pendingMinigame[39m
[31m+   const humanIsParticipant =[39m
[31m+     !!pendingMinigame && !!humanPlayer && pendingMinigame.participants.includes(humanPlayer.id)[39m
[31m+   // MinigameHost takes priority over native LOH minigame overlays when a challenge[39m
[31m+   // is pending and the human player is a participant in that challenge.[39m
[31m+   const humanIsChallengeParticipant =[39m
[31m+     !!pendingChallenge && !!humanPlayer && pendingChallenge.participants.includes(humanPlayer.id)[39m
[31m+   const showMinigameHost = humanIsChallengeParticipant[39m
[31m+   const aiOnlyChallengeResolvedRef = useRef<string | null>(null)[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const isClassicCompetitionPhase =[39m
[31m+       game.mode !== 'survival' && (game.phase === 'loh_comp' || game.phase === 'pos_comp')[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       !isClassicCompetitionPhase ||[39m
[31m+       !pendingChallenge ||[39m
[31m+       humanIsChallengeParticipant ||[39m
[31m+       pendingChallenge.participants.length === 0 ||[39m
[31m+       aiOnlyChallengeResolvedRef.current === pendingChallenge.id[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     aiOnlyChallengeResolvedRef.current = pendingChallenge.id[39m
[31m+     const rawResults = buildAiOnlyChallengeRawResults(pendingChallenge)[39m
[31m+     const scoreWinnerId = dispatch(completeChallenge(rawResults)) as string | null[39m
[31m+     const finalWinnerId = scoreWinnerId ?? pendingChallenge.participants[0][39m
[31m+     const ranked = computeScores([39m
[31m+       pendingChallenge.game.scoringAdapter,[39m
[31m+       rawResults,[39m
[31m+       pendingChallenge.game.scoringParams ?? {}[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+     const lastNonWinner = [...ranked].reverse().find((result) => result.playerId !== finalWinnerId)[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     dispatch([39m
[31m+       applyMinigameWinner({[39m
[31m+         winnerId: finalWinnerId,[39m
[31m+         lastPlaceId: lastNonWinner?.playerId ?? null,[39m
[31m+         skipSeasonUpdate: true,[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+   }, [dispatch, game.mode, game.phase, humanIsChallengeParticipant, pendingChallenge])[39m
[31m+   /** True whenever a native React LOH/POS minigame overlay should be displayed. */[39m

[31m+   )[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Ad hook: automatic interstitials (phase-based) ────────────────────────[39m
[31m+   // Each useEffect fires once per phase transition to the relevant phase.[39m
[31m+   const prevPhaseRef = useRef<string>('')[39m
[31m+   const queuePreAdAnnouncement = useCallback((placement: AdPlacement, subtitle: string) => {[39m
[31m+     pendingPreAdPlacementRef.current = placement[39m
[31m+     setPreAdAnnouncement({[39m
[31m+       key: `ad_break_${placement}`,[39m

[31m+       subtitle,[39m
[31m+       isLive: true,[39m
[31m+       autoDismissMs: 3200,[39m
[31m+     })[39m
[31m+   }, [])[39m
[31m+   const handlePreAdAnnouncementDismiss = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     const placement = pendingPreAdPlacementRef.current[39m
[31m+     pendingPreAdPlacementRef.current = null[39m
[31m+     setPreAdAnnouncement(null)[39m
[31m+     if (!placement) return[39m
[31m+     const state = storeRef.current.getState()[39m
[31m+     showInterstitial(placement, state, dispatch)[39m
[31m+   }, [dispatch])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* eslint-disable react-hooks/set-state-in-effect -- Preserve established synchronous ad-prompt timing during the orchestration extraction. */[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const prevPhase = prevPhaseRef.current[39m
[31m+     const currentPhase = game.phase[39m
[31m+     if (currentPhase === prevPhase) return[39m
[31m+     prevPhaseRef.current = currentPhase[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const state = storeRef.current.getState()[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // pos_decision_auto — every other week just before POS holder announces[39m
[31m+     // week is 1-indexed; even weeks = weeks 2, 4, 6, ...[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       currentPhase === 'pos_ceremony_results' &&[39m
[31m+       game.week % 2 === 0 &&[39m
[31m+       canShowAd('pos_decision_auto', state) &&[39m
[31m+       window.GameAds?.showInterstitial[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       const posHolderName =[39m
[31m+         game.players.find((player) => player.id === game.posWinnerId)?.name ??[39m
[31m+         'the Power of Safety holder'[39m
[31m+       queuePreAdAnnouncement([39m
[31m+         'pos_decision_auto',[39m
[31m+         `Is ${posHolderName} going to use the Power of safety to change the course of the game? Find out right after this short break!`[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // final_safety_decision_auto — before the final safety (F4 POS) holder announces[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       currentPhase === 'final4_eviction' &&[39m
[31m+       canShowAd('final_safety_decision_auto', state) &&[39m
[31m+       window.GameAds?.showInterstitial[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       queuePreAdAnnouncement([39m
[31m+         'final_safety_decision_auto',[39m
[31m+         'The final safety winner now has the deciding vote to evict. Find out who is going to be eliminated just a step before the finale. Stay with us.'[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // final_loh_decision_auto — before the final LOH (F3 Part 3 winner) announces[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       currentPhase === 'final3_decision' &&[39m
[31m+       canShowAd('final_loh_decision_auto', state) &&[39m
[31m+       window.GameAds?.showInterstitial[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       queuePreAdAnnouncement([39m
[31m+         'final_loh_decision_auto',[39m
[31m+         'The final leader of the house has to make a very important decision that might cost them the victory. Who will they choose? Find out right after the break.'[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [game.phase, game.week, game.players, game.posWinnerId, dispatch, queuePreAdAnnouncement])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Ad hook: social_energy_recharge ──────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+   // Show a rewarded prompt when the user's social energy hits 0 (once per day).[39m
[31m+   // Guards: week is not 1, not final-3 week, phase is social_1 or social_2.[39m
[31m+   const userEnergy = useAppSelector((s: RootState) =>[39m
[31m+     humanPlayer ? (s.social?.energyBank?.[humanPlayer.id] ?? 0) : 0[39m
[31m+   )[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!humanPlayer || game.mode === 'survival' || !isSocialModeEnabled(game.mode)) {[39m
[31m+       setShowEnergyRechargePrompt(false)[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const energyIsZero = userEnergy === 0[39m
[31m+     const inSocialPhase = game.phase === 'social_1' || game.phase === 'social_2'[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       !humanPlayerEliminated &&[39m
[31m+       energyIsZero &&[39m
[31m+       game.week !== 1 &&[39m
[31m+       !isFinal3Week &&[39m
[31m+       inSocialPhase[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       const state = storeRef.current.getState()[39m
[31m+       if (canShowAd('social_energy_recharge', state)) {[39m
[31m+         if (import.meta.env.DEV) {[39m
[31m+           console.log([39m
[31m+             '[ads] social_energy_recharge prompt shown — week:',[39m
[31m+             game.week,[39m
[31m+             '| phase:',[39m
[31m+             game.phase[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         setShowEnergyRechargePrompt(true)[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       if (import.meta.env.DEV && energyIsZero) {[39m
[31m+         console.log([39m
[31m+           '[ads] social_energy_recharge suppressed — week:',[39m
[31m+           game.week,[39m
[31m+           '| phase:',[39m
[31m+           game.phase,[39m
[31m+           '| isFinal3Week:',[39m
[31m+           isFinal3Week,[39m
[31m+           '| inSocialPhase:',[39m
[31m+           inSocialPhase[39m
[31m+         )[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setShowEnergyRechargePrompt(false)[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [[39m
[31m+     userEnergy,[39m
[31m+     humanPlayer,[39m
[31m+     humanPlayerEliminated,[39m
[31m+     game.mode,[39m
[31m+     game.week,[39m
[31m+     game.phase,[39m
[31m+     isFinal3Week,[39m
[31m+   ])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Ad hook: public_meter_disliked_boost ──────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+   // Show a rewarded prompt when the user's approval drops below 40%[39m
[31m+   // (disliked or worse), at most once per day.[39m
[31m+   const userApproval = useAppSelector((s: RootState) =>[39m
[31m+     humanPlayer ? (s.publicOpinion?.profiles?.[humanPlayer.id]?.approval ?? 100) : 100[39m
[31m+   )[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!humanPlayer || game.mode === 'survival' || game.publicModeEnabled !== true) {[39m
[31m+       setShowDislikedBoostPrompt(false)[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const todayIsoDate = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       !humanPlayerEliminated &&[39m
[31m+       shouldShowDislikedBoostPrompt(userApproval, lastDislikedPromptDate, todayIsoDate)[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       const state = storeRef.current.getState()[39m
[31m+       if (canShowAd('public_meter_disliked_boost', state)) {[39m
[31m+         setLastDislikedPromptDate(todayIsoDate)[39m
[31m+         setShowDislikedBoostPrompt(true)[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // Auto-dismiss if approval recovered above disliked threshold.[39m
[31m+     // Keep the last shown date so the prompt does not reappear again the same day[39m
[31m+     // if approval dips back into the disliked band.[39m
[31m+     if (userApproval > DISLIKED_MAX_APPROVAL) {[39m
[31m+       setShowDislikedBoostPrompt(false)[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [[39m
[31m+     adsState?.dailyUsage?.public_meter_disliked_boost,[39m
[31m+     humanPlayer,[39m
[31m+     humanPlayerEliminated,[39m
[31m+     game.mode,[39m
[31m+     game.publicModeEnabled,[39m
[31m+     lastDislikedPromptDate,[39m
[31m+     setLastDislikedPromptDate,[39m
[31m+     userApproval,[39m
[31m+   ])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* eslint-enable react-hooks/set-state-in-effect */[39m
[31m+   // ── Social phase panel ────────────────────────────────────────────────────[39m

[31m+   const SOCIAL_INTERACTION_PHASES = new Set<string>([[39m

[31m+     'loh_results',[39m
[31m+     'social_1',[39m
[31m+     'nominations',[39m
[31m+     'nomination_results',[39m

[31m+     'pos_ceremony_results',[39m
[31m+     'social_2',[39m
[31m+   ])[39m
[31m+   const isSocialPhase = SOCIAL_INTERACTION_PHASES.has(game.phase)[39m
[31m+   const showSocialPanel = isSocialPhase && !!humanPlayer && isSocialModeEnabled(game.mode)[39m

[31m+   // The individual controllers publish presentation signals; this coordinator[39m

[31m+     game.awaitingFinal3Plea === true && game.phase === 'final3_decision' && !!game.lohId[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+   const flowCoordination = coordinateGameFlows({[39m

[31m+       presentation: {[39m
[31m+         blocksControls: [[39m
[31m+           showEnergyRechargePrompt,[39m
[31m+           showDislikedBoostPrompt,[39m

[31m+   const { showGameControlDock, awaitingHumanDecision } = flowCoordination[39m

[31m+     // Fall back to the remote-config headline when no phase-specific message applies.[39m
[31m+     return remoteMainTvHeadline ?? undefined[39m
[31m+   }, [game.phase, game.awaitingHumanVote, activeConfessionalDecision, remoteMainTvHeadline])[39m

[31m+     })[39m
[31m+   }[39m

[31m+       autoDismissMs: SOCIAL_MODULE_UNAVAILABLE_ANNOUNCEMENT_MS,[39m

[31m+   const responsiveGameLayout = useResponsiveGameLayout(gameScreenRef, {[39m

[31m+[39m

[31m+ // Exported only as a pure regression-test seam; it does not participate in Fast Refresh state.[39m
[31m+ // eslint-disable-next-line react-refresh/only-export-components[39m
[31m+ export function buildTieBreakPitch(relationship: number, playerId: string, week: number): string {[39m
[31m+   const alternate =[39m
[31m+     Math.abs(`${playerId}:${week}`.split('').reduce((sum, char) => sum + char.charCodeAt(0), 0)) %[39m
[31m+       2 ===[39m
[31m+     0[39m
[31m+   if (relationship >= 45)[39m
[31m+     return alternate[39m
[31m+       ? 'We have protected each other before. Keep me, and I will return it.'[39m
[31m+       : 'Our relationship is real. Do not let one tied vote end it.'[39m
[31m+   if (relationship >= 15)[39m
[31m+     return alternate[39m
[31m+       ? 'I can still be a number for you after tonight. Give me that chance.'[39m
[31m+       : 'Keep me and you keep an option in this house?not another enemy.'[39m
[31m+   if (relationship <= -20)[39m
[31m+     return alternate[39m
[31m+       ? 'We are not close, but eliminating me only finishes someone else?s move.'[39m
[31m+       : 'You do not have to trust me to see I am useful as a shield.'[39m
[31m+   return alternate[39m
[31m+     ? 'Give me one more day and judge me by what I do with it.'[39m
[31m+     : 'This decision is yours. I am asking you not to make me the easy answer.'[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ function buildAiOnlyChallengeRawResults(challenge: PendingChallenge) {[39m

[31m+     playerId: id,[39m
[31m+     rawValue: challenge.aiScores[id] ?? 0,[39m
[31m+     ...(challenge.aiTiebreakers?.[id] != null ? { tiebreaker: challenge.aiTiebreakers[id] } : {}),[39m
[31m+   }))[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+   function playerToHouseguest(p: Player) {[39m
[31m+     const isEvicted = p.status === 'evicted' || p.status === 'jury'[39m
[31m+     const parts: string[] = [][39m
[31m+     const povProtectedIds = new Set(game.povProtectedIds ?? [])[39m
[31m+     if (game.lohId === p.id) parts.push('loh')[39m
[31m+     if (game.posWinnerId === p.id) parts.push('pos')[39m
[31m+     if (povProtectedIds.has(p.id)) parts.push('veto_safe')[39m
[31m+     // Suppress permanent nomination badge while the nomination animation is[39m
[31m+     // playing — otherwise AI-LOH nominees (already in game.nomineeIds) would[39m
[31m+     // show the permanent ❓ badge before the animated badge lands.[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingNominee = showNomAnim && nomAnimPlayers.some((n) => n.id === p.id)[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingSaveTarget = pendingSaveCeremony?.savedId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const isPublicSaveWinner = pendingPublicSaveResult?.savedId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingReplacementNominee = activeReplacementAnimationTargetId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const isAnimatingAwardWinner =[39m
[31m+       pendingWinnerCeremony?.winnerId === p.id || (showAdvanceHohCeremony && game.lohId === p.id)[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       Array.isArray(game.nomineeIds) &&[39m
[31m+       game.nomineeIds.includes(p.id) &&[39m
[31m+       !isAnimatingNominee &&[39m
[31m+       !isAnimatingSaveTarget &&[39m
[31m+       !isPublicSaveWinner &&[39m
[31m+       !isAnimatingReplacementNominee[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       parts.push('nominated')[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (p.status === 'jury') parts.push('jury')[39m
[31m+     // When suppressing the nominated badge, also guard the p.status fallback so[39m
[31m+     // that players whose p.status is already 'nominated' (AI-committed nominees)[39m
[31m+     // don't have that status leak through when parts is empty.[39m
[31m+     const suppressFallbackStatus =[39m
[31m+       isAnimatingNominee ||[39m
[31m+       isAnimatingSaveTarget ||[39m
[31m+       isPublicSaveWinner ||[39m
[31m+       isAnimatingReplacementNominee[39m
[31m+     const statuses =[39m
[31m+       parts.length > 0[39m
[31m+         ? parts.join('+')[39m
[31m+         : suppressFallbackStatus[39m
[31m+           ? 'active'[39m
[31m+           : (p.status ?? 'active')[39m
[31m+     const isReturning = battleBackReturnId === p.id[39m
[31m+     const nominationCeremonyState: 'loh' | 'danger' | 'locked' | undefined =[39m
[31m+       !isEvicted && showNominationDangerSignals[39m
[31m+         ? game.lohId === p.id[39m
[31m+           ? 'loh'[39m
[31m+           : nominationDangerLockedId === p.id[39m
[31m+             ? 'locked'[39m
[31m+             : 'danger'[39m
[31m+         : undefined[39m
[31m+     return {[39m
[31m+       id: p.id,[39m
[31m+       name: p.name,[39m
[31m+       avatarUrl: getProfilePhotoAvatarId(p.avatar) ? p.avatar : resolveAvatar(p),[39m
[31m+       statuses,[39m
[31m+       finalRank: (p.finalRank ?? null) as 1 | 2 | 3 | null,[39m
[31m+       isEvicted,[39m
[31m+       isYou: p.isUser,[39m
[31m+       // The landing badge is the only award badge visible during a winner[39m
[31m+       // ceremony. This also covers the outgoing-LOH path, where the reducer has[39m
[31m+       // already committed the winner before the overlay starts.[39m
[31m+       showPermanentBadge: !isAnimatingNominee && !isAnimatingAwardWinner,[39m
[31m+       nominationCeremonyState,[39m
[31m+       layoutId: `avatar-tile-${p.id}`,[39m
[31m+       isEvicting:[39m
[31m+         (showEvictionSplash && pendingEvictionPlayer?.id === p.id) ||[39m
[31m+         game.evictionOverlayPlayerId === p.id ||[39m
[31m+         isReturning,[39m
[31m+       onClick: () => handleAvatarSelect(p),[39m
[31m+       onHoldPreviewStart: () => setPreviewPlayer(p),[39m
[31m+       onHoldPreviewEnd: () =>[39m
[31m+         setPreviewPlayer((current) => (current?.id === p.id ? null : current)),[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m

[31m+   // Shown when a LOH or POS competition is in progress and the human player[39m

[31m+   // MinigameHost takes priority over native LOH minigame overlays when a challenge[39m

[31m+   const humanIsChallengeParticipant =[39m
[31m+     !!pendingChallenge && !!humanPlayer && pendingChallenge.participants.includes(humanPlayer.id)[39m
[31m+   const showMinigameHost = humanIsChallengeParticipant[39m
[31m+   const aiOnlyChallengeResolvedRef = useRef<string | null>(null)[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const isClassicCompetitionPhase =[39m
[31m+       game.mode !== 'survival' && (game.phase === 'loh_comp' || game.phase === 'pos_comp')[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       !isClassicCompetitionPhase ||[39m
[31m+       !pendingChallenge ||[39m
[31m+       humanIsChallengeParticipant ||[39m
[31m+       pendingChallenge.participants.length === 0 ||[39m
[31m+       aiOnlyChallengeResolvedRef.current === pendingChallenge.id[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     aiOnlyChallengeResolvedRef.current = pendingChallenge.id[39m
[31m+     const rawResults = buildAiOnlyChallengeRawResults(pendingChallenge)[39m
[31m+     const scoreWinnerId = dispatch(completeChallenge(rawResults)) as string | null[39m
[31m+     const finalWinnerId = scoreWinnerId ?? pendingChallenge.participants[0][39m
[31m+     const ranked = computeScores([39m
[31m+       pendingChallenge.game.scoringAdapter,[39m
[31m+       rawResults,[39m
[31m+       pendingChallenge.game.scoringParams ?? {}[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+     const lastNonWinner = [...ranked].reverse().find((result) => result.playerId !== finalWinnerId)[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     dispatch([39m
[31m+       applyMinigameWinner({[39m
[31m+         winnerId: finalWinnerId,[39m
[31m+         lastPlaceId: lastNonWinner?.playerId ?? null,[39m
[31m+         skipSeasonUpdate: true,[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+     )[39m

[31m+   )[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Ad hook: automatic interstitials (phase-based) ────────────────────────[39m
[31m+   // Each useEffect fires once per phase transition to the relevant phase.[39m
[31m+   const prevPhaseRef = useRef<string>('')[39m
[31m+   const queuePreAdAnnouncement = useCallback((placement: AdPlacement, subtitle: string) => {[39m
[31m+     pendingPreAdPlacementRef.current = placement[39m
[31m+     setPreAdAnnouncement({[39m
[31m+       key: `ad_break_${placement}`,[39m
[31m+       title: 'SHORT BREAK',[39m
[31m+       subtitle,[39m
[31m+       isLive: true,[39m
[31m+       autoDismissMs: 3200,[39m
[31m+     })[39m
[31m+   }, [])[39m
[31m+   const handlePreAdAnnouncementDismiss = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     const placement = pendingPreAdPlacementRef.current[39m
[31m+     pendingPreAdPlacementRef.current = null[39m
[31m+     setPreAdAnnouncement(null)[39m
[31m+     if (!placement) return[39m
[31m+     const state = storeRef.current.getState()[39m
[31m+     showInterstitial(placement, state, dispatch)[39m
[31m+   }, [dispatch])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /* eslint-disable react-hooks/set-state-in-effect -- Preserve established synchronous ad-prompt timing during the orchestration extraction. */[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const prevPhase = prevPhaseRef.current[39m
[31m+     const currentPhase = game.phase[39m
[31m+     if (currentPhase === prevPhase) return[39m
[31m+     prevPhaseRef.current = currentPhase[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const state = storeRef.current.getState()[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // pos_decision_auto — every other week just before POS holder announces[39m
[31m+     // week is 1-indexed; even weeks = weeks 2, 4, 6, ...[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       currentPhase === 'pos_ceremony_results' &&[39m
[31m+       game.week % 2 === 0 &&[39m
[31m+       canShowAd('pos_decision_auto', state) &&[39m
[31m+       window.GameAds?.showInterstitial[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       const posHolderName =[39m
[31m+         game.players.find((player) => player.id === game.posWinnerId)?.name ??[39m
[31m+         'the Power of Safety holder'[39m
[31m+       queuePreAdAnnouncement([39m
[31m+         'pos_decision_auto',[39m
[31m+         `Is ${posHolderName} going to use the Power of safety to change the course of the game? Find out right after this short break!`[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // final_safety_decision_auto — before the final safety (F4 POS) holder announces[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       currentPhase === 'final4_eviction' &&[39m
[31m+       canShowAd('final_safety_decision_auto', state) &&[39m
[31m+       window.GameAds?.showInterstitial[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       queuePreAdAnnouncement([39m
[31m+         'final_safety_decision_auto',[39m

[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // final_loh_decision_auto — before the final LOH (F3 Part 3 winner) announces[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       currentPhase === 'final3_decision' &&[39m
[31m+       canShowAd('final_loh_decision_auto', state) &&[39m
[31m+       window.GameAds?.showInterstitial[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       queuePreAdAnnouncement([39m
[31m+         'final_loh_decision_auto',[39m
[31m+         'The final leader of the house has to make a very important decision that might cost them the victory. Who will they choose? Find out right after the break.'[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [game.phase, game.week, game.players, game.posWinnerId, dispatch, queuePreAdAnnouncement])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Ad hook: social_energy_recharge ──────────────────────────────────────[39m
[31m+   // Show a rewarded prompt when the user's social energy hits 0 (once per day).[39m
[31m+   // Guards: week is not 1, not final-3 week, phase is social_1 or social_2.[39m
[31m+   const userEnergy = useAppSelector((s: RootState) =>[39m
[31m+     humanPlayer ? (s.social?.energyBank?.[humanPlayer.id] ?? 0) : 0[39m

[31m+       game.week !== 1 &&[39m
[31m+       !isFinal3Week &&[39m
[31m+       inSocialPhase[39m

[31m+       if (canShowAd('social_energy_recharge', state)) {[39m
[31m+         if (import.meta.env.DEV) {[39m
[31m+           console.log([39m
[31m+             '[ads] social_energy_recharge prompt shown — week:',[39m
[31m+             game.week,[39m
[31m+             '| phase:',[39m
[31m+             game.phase[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         setShowEnergyRechargePrompt(true)[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       if (import.meta.env.DEV && energyIsZero) {[39m
[31m+         console.log([39m
[31m+           '[ads] social_energy_recharge suppressed — week:',[39m

[31m+         )[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setShowEnergyRechargePrompt(false)[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [[39m
[31m+     userEnergy,[39m
[31m+     humanPlayer,[39m
[31m+     humanPlayerEliminated,[39m
[31m+     game.mode,[39m
[31m+     game.week,[39m

[31m+   ])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // ── Ad hook: public_meter_disliked_boost ──────────────────────────────────[39m

[31m+         setShowDislikedBoostPrompt(true)[39m
[31m+       }[39m

[31m+     // Auto-dismiss if approval recovered above disliked threshold.[39m

[31m+       setShowDislikedBoostPrompt(false)[39m

[31m+     adsState?.dailyUsage?.public_meter_disliked_boost,[39m

[31m+     lastDislikedPromptDate,[39m

[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * CeremonyOverlay — spotlight cutout overlay for ceremony moments.[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * Renders on top of the LIVE GameScreen with:[39m
[31m+  *   • A full-screen dim layer (SVG mask) that punches rounded holes over[39m
[31m+  *     the target tile(s), keeping them visible and highlighted.[39m
[31m+  *   • Badge emoji(s) that animate from a start position (screen centre or[39m
[31m+  *     from another tile) and land onto the target tile(s).[39m
[31m+  *   • A caption below/above the cutouts with ceremony text.[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * Defensive fallback: when `tiles` are empty or all rects are null/zero[39m
[31m+  * (headless / jsdom), `onDone` fires immediately (no timers) and nothing[39m
[31m+  * renders — callers commit state without animation.[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * Usage:[39m
[31m+  *   <CeremonyOverlay[39m
[31m+  *     tiles={[{ rect, badge: '👑', badgeStart: 'center' }]}[39m
[31m+  *     caption="Taylor wins Leader of the House!"[39m
[31m+  *     onDone={handleDone}[39m
[31m+  *   />[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from 'react'[39m
[31m+ import './CeremonyOverlay.css'[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export interface CeremonyTile {[39m
[31m+   /** Bounding rect of the target tile. null = skip this tile. */[39m
[31m+   rect: DOMRect | null[39m
[31m+   /** Badge emoji to animate onto this tile (e.g. '👑', '🛡️', '❓'). */[39m
[31m+   badge?: string[39m
[31m+   /** Optional badge image source rendered instead of badge text. */[39m
[31m+   badgeImageSrc?: string[39m
[31m+   /** Optional role/context label shown as a pill above the spotlighted tile. */[39m
[31m+   label?: string[39m
[31m+   /** Optional glow tone for the spotlight ring. */[39m
[31m+   glowTone?: 'gold' | 'danger' | 'warning' | 'success'[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Where the badge starts before flying to the tile:[39m
[31m+    *   'center' — screen centre (default for winner badges)[39m
[31m+    *   DOMRect  — another tile's rect (for badge transfers)[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   badgeStart?: 'center' | DOMRect[39m
[31m+   /** Optional ARIA label for the badge */[39m
[31m+   badgeLabel?: string[39m
[31m+   /** Optional badge motion style. Defaults to landing onto the tile. */[39m
[31m+   badgeMotion?: 'land' | 'extract'[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export interface CeremonyOverlayProps {[39m
[31m+   /** Tiles to spotlight (1–3 tiles) */[39m
[31m+   tiles: CeremonyTile[][39m
[31m+   /** Caption text shown below the spotlighted tiles */[39m
[31m+   caption: string[39m
[31m+   /** Optional subtitle below caption */[39m
[31m+   subtitle?: string[39m
[31m+   /** Called when animation completes (or immediately when rects missing) */[39m
[31m+   onDone: () => void[39m
[31m+   /** Total visible duration in ms before exit begins (default 2800) */[39m
[31m+   durationMs?: number[39m
[31m+   /** ARIA label for the overlay */[39m
[31m+   ariaLabel?: string[39m
[31m+   /** When false, skips rendering the dim cutout layer. Defaults to true. */[39m
[31m+   showDim?: boolean[39m
[31m+   /** When false, skips rendering the caption/subtitle block. Defaults to true. */[39m
[31m+   showCaption?: boolean[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Optional callback to resolve tile rects lazily (after DOM commit).[39m
[31m+    * When provided, called once on mount and the returned tiles replace[39m
[31m+    * the `tiles` prop.  Useful when tile DOM elements aren't available[39m
[31m+    * during the render phase (e.g. first paint).[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   resolveTiles?: () => CeremonyTile[][39m
[31m+   /** Changes when the surrounding layout budget changes and tile rects should be refreshed. */[39m
[31m+   layoutSignal?: string | number[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** Badge animation phases with timing (ms from overlay mount) */[39m
[31m+ const APPEAR_DELAY = 200[39m
[31m+ const APPEAR_DURATION = 450[39m
[31m+ const FLY_DELAY = APPEAR_DELAY + APPEAR_DURATION // 650[39m
[31m+ const FLY_DURATION = 500[39m
[31m+ const LAND_DELAY = FLY_DELAY + FLY_DURATION // 1150[39m
[31m+ const LAND_DURATION = 350[39m
[31m+ const HOLD_DELAY = LAND_DELAY + LAND_DURATION // 1500[39m
[31m+ const COMPACT_LABEL_BREAKPOINT = '(max-width: 560px)'[39m
[31m+ const COMPACT_TILE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {[39m
[31m+   'LOH Nominee': 'NOMINEE',[39m
[31m+   'Last in LOH Comp': 'LAST PLACE',[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ // Keep a small viewport margin so pills never clip against the screen edge.[39m
[31m+ const LABEL_EDGE_MARGIN = 12[39m
[31m+ // Default vertical anchor for pills positioned just above a spotlight cutout.[39m
[31m+ const LABEL_BASE_TOP = 30[39m
[31m+ // Minimum pill widths keep short labels visually balanced.[39m
[31m+ const MIN_COMPACT_LABEL_WIDTH = 78[39m
[31m+ const MIN_FULL_LABEL_WIDTH = 96[39m
[31m+ // Approximate character widths used only for collision estimation.[39m
[31m+ const COMPACT_LABEL_CHAR_WIDTH = 7[39m
[31m+ const FULL_LABEL_CHAR_WIDTH = 7.8[39m
[31m+ const LABEL_WIDTH_PADDING = 26[39m
[31m+ const LABEL_HEIGHT = 24[39m
[31m+ // Near the top edge, pills can only stack downward without clipping.[39m
[31m+ const LABEL_STACKED_THRESHOLD = 18[39m
[31m+ const LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_STEP = 28[39m
[31m+ const LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_MAX = 56[39m
[31m+ // Extraction motion lifts the badge just above the tile without jumping into[39m
[31m+ // the TV chrome near the top edge on compact mobile layouts.[39m
[31m+ const BADGE_EXTRACT_Y_OFFSET = 28[39m
[31m+ const BADGE_EXTRACT_MIN_TOP = 12[39m
[31m+ // Safe small-screen fallback dimensions for non-browser/SSR rendering paths.[39m
[31m+ const SSR_VIEWPORT_WIDTH = 390[39m
[31m+ const SSR_VIEWPORT_HEIGHT = 844[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ interface LabelLayout {[39m
[31m+   key: string[39m
[31m+   label: string[39m
[31m+   left: number[39m
[31m+   top: number[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function getDisplayTileLabel(label: string, useCompactTileLabels: boolean): string {[39m
[31m+   if (!useCompactTileLabels) return label[39m
[31m+   return COMPACT_TILE_LABELS[label] ?? label[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function labelRectsOverlap([39m
[31m+   leftA: number,[39m
[31m+   topA: number,[39m
[31m+   widthA: number,[39m
[31m+   heightA: number,[39m
[31m+   leftB: number,[39m
[31m+   topB: number,[39m
[31m+   widthB: number,[39m
[31m+   heightB: number,[39m
[31m+   gap = 8[39m
[31m+ ): boolean {[39m
[31m+   // leftA/leftB are center x-coordinates because pills are positioned with[39m
[31m+   // translateX(-50%) in CSS.[39m
[31m+   const aLeft = leftA - widthA / 2[39m
[31m+   const aRight = leftA + widthA / 2[39m
[31m+   const bLeft = leftB - widthB / 2[39m
[31m+   const bRight = leftB + widthB / 2[39m
[31m+   const horizontalOverlap = aLeft < bRight + gap && aRight + gap > bLeft[39m
[31m+   const verticalOverlap = topA < topB + heightB + gap && topA + heightA + gap > topB[39m
[31m+   return horizontalOverlap && verticalOverlap[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function calculateLabelWidth(label: string, useCompactTileLabels: boolean): number {[39m
[31m+   const minWidth = useCompactTileLabels ? MIN_COMPACT_LABEL_WIDTH : MIN_FULL_LABEL_WIDTH[39m
[31m+   const charWidth = useCompactTileLabels ? COMPACT_LABEL_CHAR_WIDTH : FULL_LABEL_CHAR_WIDTH[39m
[31m+   return Math.max(minWidth, label.length * charWidth + LABEL_WIDTH_PADDING)[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function isTileBadgeOrigin(badgeStart: CeremonyTile['badgeStart']): badgeStart is DOMRect {[39m
[31m+   return badgeStart != null && badgeStart !== 'center'[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ type BadgePhase = 'hidden' | 'appearing' | 'flying' | 'landed' | 'holding'[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** Cutout padding (px) around each tile rect */[39m
[31m+ const CUTOUT_PAD = 6[39m
[31m+ const CUTOUT_RADIUS = 10[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export default function CeremonyOverlay({[39m
[31m+   tiles: tilesProp,[39m
[31m+   caption,[39m
[31m+   subtitle,[39m
[31m+   onDone,[39m
[31m+   durationMs = 2800,[39m
[31m+   ariaLabel,[39m
[31m+   showDim = true,[39m
[31m+   showCaption = true,[39m
[31m+   resolveTiles,[39m
[31m+   layoutSignal,[39m
[31m+ }: CeremonyOverlayProps) {[39m
[31m+   const [visible, setVisible] = useState(true)[39m
[31m+   const [badgePhase, setBadgePhase] = useState<BadgePhase>('hidden')[39m
[31m+   const timersRef = useRef<number[]>([])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Lazily resolve tiles: if resolveTiles is provided, use it on mount[39m
[31m+   // (after DOM commit) to get accurate DOMRects. Otherwise use tilesProp.[39m
[31m+   const [resolvedTiles, setResolvedTiles] = useState<CeremonyTile[] | null>([39m
[31m+     resolveTiles ? null : tilesProp[39m
[31m+   )[39m
[31m+   const [useCompactTileLabels, setUseCompactTileLabels] = useState(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return false[39m
[31m+     return window.matchMedia(COMPACT_LABEL_BREAKPOINT).matches[39m
[31m+   })[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const tiles = resolvedTiles ?? tilesProp[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return undefined[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia(COMPACT_LABEL_BREAKPOINT)[39m
[31m+     const handleChange = (event: MediaQueryListEvent | MediaQueryList) => {[39m
[31m+       setUseCompactTileLabels(event.matches)[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     handleChange(mediaQuery)[39m
[31m+     if (typeof mediaQuery.addEventListener === 'function') {[39m
[31m+       mediaQuery.addEventListener('change', handleChange)[39m
[31m+     } else if (typeof mediaQuery.addListener === 'function') {[39m
[31m+       mediaQuery.addListener(handleChange)[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return () => {[39m
[31m+       if (typeof mediaQuery.removeEventListener === 'function') {[39m
[31m+         mediaQuery.removeEventListener('change', handleChange)[39m
[31m+       } else if (typeof mediaQuery.removeListener === 'function') {[39m
[31m+         mediaQuery.removeListener(handleChange)[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Validate: at least one tile with a non-zero rect[39m
[31m+   const validTiles = tiles.filter((t) => t.rect != null && (t.rect.width > 0 || t.rect.height > 0))[39m
[31m+   const hasValidTiles = validTiles.length > 0[39m
[31m+   // Track whether we're still waiting for resolveTiles to run[39m
[31m+   const pendingResolve = resolveTiles != null && resolvedTiles === null[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const clearTimers = useCallback(() => {[39m
[31m+     timersRef.current.forEach((id) => window.clearTimeout(id))[39m
[31m+     timersRef.current = [][39m
[31m+   }, [])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const addTimer = useCallback((fn: () => void, ms: number) => {[39m
[31m+     const id = window.setTimeout(fn, ms)[39m
[31m+     timersRef.current.push(id)[39m
[31m+     return id[39m
[31m+   }, [])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Resolve tiles lazily and refresh them when the measured layout changes.[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (resolveTiles) {[39m
[31m+       setResolvedTiles(resolveTiles())[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [layoutSignal])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Headless fallback: fire onDone immediately[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     // Wait for tiles to be resolved before starting animation.[39m
[31m+     if (pendingResolve) return[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (!hasValidTiles) {[39m
[31m+       onDone()[39m
[31m+       return[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // Badge animation timeline[39m
[31m+     addTimer(() => setBadgePhase('appearing'), APPEAR_DELAY)[39m
[31m+     addTimer(() => setBadgePhase('flying'), FLY_DELAY)[39m
[31m+     addTimer(() => setBadgePhase('landed'), LAND_DELAY)[39m
[31m+     addTimer(() => setBadgePhase('holding'), HOLD_DELAY)[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // Exit sequence[39m
[31m+     addTimer(() => {[39m
[31m+       setVisible(false)[39m
[31m+       addTimer(onDone, 350)[39m
[31m+     }, durationMs)[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return clearTimers[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [hasValidTiles, durationMs, pendingResolve])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Compute cutout rects for the SVG mask[39m
[31m+   const cutouts = validTiles.map((t) => {[39m
[31m+     const r = t.rect![39m
[31m+     return {[39m
[31m+       x: r.left - CUTOUT_PAD,[39m
[31m+       y: r.top - CUTOUT_PAD,[39m
[31m+       w: r.width + CUTOUT_PAD * 2,[39m
[31m+       h: r.height + CUTOUT_PAD * 2,[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   })[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const viewportWidth = typeof window === 'undefined' ? SSR_VIEWPORT_WIDTH : window.innerWidth[39m
[31m+   const viewportHeight = typeof window === 'undefined' ? SSR_VIEWPORT_HEIGHT : window.innerHeight[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Caption placement: below the lowest cutout[39m
[31m+   const maxBottom = cutouts.length > 0 ? Math.max(...cutouts.map((c) => c.y + c.h)) : 0[39m
[31m+   const captionTop = Math.min(maxBottom + 16, viewportHeight - 80)[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Badge start/target positions[39m
[31m+   const badgePositions = validTiles.map((t) => {[39m
[31m+     const r = t.rect![39m
[31m+     // Left-side anchor: align with .badgeStack { top: 4px; left: 4px } in AvatarTile.[39m
[31m+     // Badge uses transform translate(-50%, -100%), so targetX = badge center x,[39m
[31m+     // targetY = badge bottom y. Permanent badge center ≈ tile.left+14, bottom ≈ tile.top+24.[39m
[31m+     const targetX = r.left + 14[39m
[31m+     const targetY = r.top + 24[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     let startX: number[39m
[31m+     let startY: number[39m
[31m+     let endY = targetY[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (t.badgeMotion === 'extract') {[39m
[31m+       startX = targetX[39m
[31m+       startY = targetY[39m
[31m+       endY = Math.max(BADGE_EXTRACT_MIN_TOP, targetY - BADGE_EXTRACT_Y_OFFSET)[39m
[31m+     } else if (t.badgeStart && t.badgeStart !== 'center' && 'left' in t.badgeStart) {[39m
[31m+       // Transfer from another tile[39m
[31m+       startX = t.badgeStart.left + t.badgeStart.width / 2[39m
[31m+       startY = t.badgeStart.top[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       // Centre of viewport[39m
[31m+       startX = viewportWidth / 2[39m
[31m+       startY = viewportHeight / 2[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return { startX, startY, targetX, targetY, endY }[39m
[31m+   })[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Badge current position based on phase[39m
[31m+   const getBadgeStyle = (idx: number): React.CSSProperties => {[39m
[31m+     const pos = badgePositions[idx][39m
[31m+     const badgeMotion = validTiles[idx]?.badgeMotion ?? 'land'[39m
[31m+     switch (badgePhase) {[39m
[31m+       case 'hidden':[39m
[31m+         return { left: pos.startX, top: pos.startY, opacity: 0 }[39m
[31m+       case 'appearing':[39m
[31m+         return { left: pos.startX, top: pos.startY }[39m
[31m+       case 'flying':[39m
[31m+         if (badgeMotion === 'extract') {[39m
[31m+           return { left: pos.targetX, top: pos.endY }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         return { left: pos.targetX, top: pos.targetY }[39m
[31m+       case 'landed':[39m
[31m+       case 'holding':[39m
[31m+         if (badgeMotion === 'extract') {[39m
[31m+           return { left: pos.targetX, top: pos.endY }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         return { left: pos.targetX, top: pos.targetY }[39m
[31m+       default:[39m
[31m+         return { left: pos.startX, top: pos.startY }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getBadgeClass = (phase: BadgePhase) => {[39m
[31m+     if (phase === 'hidden') return ''[39m
[31m+     return `ceremony-overlay__badge--${phase}`[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const labelLayouts = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     return validTiles.reduce<LabelLayout[]>((layouts, tile, i) => {[39m
[31m+       if (!tile.label) return layouts[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const cutout = cutouts[i][39m
[31m+       const displayLabel = getDisplayTileLabel(tile.label, useCompactTileLabels)[39m
[31m+       const estimatedWidth = calculateLabelWidth(displayLabel, useCompactTileLabels)[39m
[31m+       const clampedLeft = Math.min([39m
[31m+         Math.max(cutout.x + cutout.w / 2, estimatedWidth / 2 + LABEL_EDGE_MARGIN),[39m
[31m+         viewportWidth - estimatedWidth / 2 - LABEL_EDGE_MARGIN[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       const baseTop = Math.max(cutout.y - LABEL_BASE_TOP, LABEL_EDGE_MARGIN)[39m
[31m+       // If the base position is already near the top viewport edge, only move[39m
[31m+       // downward to avoid clipping the pill off-screen. Otherwise allow both[39m
[31m+       // upward and downward staggering to resolve collisions.[39m
[31m+       const offsets =[39m
[31m+         baseTop <= LABEL_STACKED_THRESHOLD[39m
[31m+           ? [0, LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_STEP, LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_MAX][39m
[31m+           : [[39m
[31m+               0,[39m
[31m+               -LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_STEP,[39m
[31m+               LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_STEP,[39m
[31m+               -LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_MAX,[39m
[31m+               LABEL_VERTICAL_OFFSET_MAX,[39m
[31m+             ][39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       let placedTop = baseTop[39m
[31m+       for (const offset of offsets) {[39m
[31m+         const candidateTop = Math.max(LABEL_EDGE_MARGIN, baseTop + offset)[39m
[31m+         const collides = layouts.some((layout) =>[39m
[31m+           labelRectsOverlap([39m
[31m+             clampedLeft,[39m
[31m+             candidateTop,[39m
[31m+             estimatedWidth,[39m
[31m+             LABEL_HEIGHT,[39m
[31m+             layout.left,[39m
[31m+             layout.top,[39m
[31m+             calculateLabelWidth(layout.label, useCompactTileLabels),[39m
[31m+             LABEL_HEIGHT[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         )[39m
[31m+         if (!collides) {[39m
[31m+           placedTop = candidateTop[39m
[31m+           break[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       layouts.push({[39m
[31m+         key: `label-${i}`,[39m
[31m+         label: displayLabel,[39m
[31m+         left: clampedLeft,[39m
[31m+         top: placedTop,[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       return layouts[39m
[31m+     }, [])[39m
[31m+   }, [cutouts, useCompactTileLabels, validTiles, viewportWidth])[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (pendingResolve || !hasValidTiles) return null[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <>[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className={`ceremony-overlay ${visible ? 'ceremony-overlay--visible' : 'ceremony-overlay--exiting'}`}[39m
[31m+         role="status"[39m
[31m+         aria-live="assertive"[39m
[31m+         aria-label={ariaLabel ?? caption}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         {/* SVG dim layer with mask cutouts */}[39m
[31m+         {showDim && ([39m
[31m+           <div className="ceremony-overlay__dim">[39m
[31m+             <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">[39m
[31m+               <defs>[39m
[31m+                 <mask id="ceremony-cutout-mask">[39m
[31m+                   {/* White fill = fully dimmed */}[39m
[31m+                   <rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="white" />[39m
[31m+                   {/* Black rects = cutout holes (transparent in the dim) */}[39m
[31m+                   {cutouts.map((c, i) => ([39m
[31m+                     <rect[39m
[31m+                       key={i}[39m
[31m+                       x={c.x}[39m
[31m+                       y={c.y}[39m
[31m+                       width={c.w}[39m
[31m+                       height={c.h}[39m
[31m+                       rx={CUTOUT_RADIUS}[39m
[31m+                       ry={CUTOUT_RADIUS}[39m
[31m+                       fill="black"[39m
[31m+                     />[39m
[31m+                   ))}[39m
[31m+                 </mask>[39m
[31m+               </defs>[39m
[31m+               <rect[39m
[31m+                 width="100%"[39m
[31m+                 height="100%"[39m
[31m+                 fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.78)"[39m
[31m+                 mask="url(#ceremony-cutout-mask)"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </svg>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Glow rings around cutout tiles */}[39m
[31m+         {cutouts.map((c, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             key={i}[39m
[31m+             className={`ceremony-overlay__glow ceremony-overlay__glow--${validTiles[i]?.glowTone ?? 'gold'}`}[39m
[31m+             style={{ left: c.x, top: c.y, width: c.w, height: c.h }}[39m
[31m+             data-ceremony-tone={validTiles[i]?.glowTone ?? 'gold'}[39m
[31m+             aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Optional role/context pills above spotlighted tiles */}[39m
[31m+         {labelLayouts.map((layout) => ([39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             key={layout.key}[39m
[31m+             className="ceremony-overlay__tile-label"[39m
[31m+             style={{ left: layout.left, top: layout.top }}[39m
[31m+             aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {layout.label}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {/* Caption text */}[39m
[31m+         {showCaption && ([39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className={`ceremony-overlay__caption ${visible ? 'ceremony-overlay__caption--visible' : ''}`}[39m
[31m+             style={{ top: captionTop }}[39m
[31m+             aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <p className="ceremony-overlay__caption-text">{caption}</p>[39m
[31m+             {subtitle && <p className="ceremony-overlay__caption-sub">{subtitle}</p>}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Animated badges — placed outside the dim container so they render above the mask */}[39m
[31m+       {validTiles.map((t, i) => {[39m
[31m+         if (!t.badge) return null[39m
[31m+         return ([39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             key={i}[39m
[31m+             className={`ceremony-overlay__badge ${getBadgeClass(badgePhase)}`}[39m
[31m+             style={{[39m
[31m+               ...getBadgeStyle(i),[39m
[31m+               zIndex: 8701,[39m
[31m+               position: 'fixed',[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+             data-badge-origin={isTileBadgeOrigin(t.badgeStart) ? 'tile' : 'center'}[39m
[31m+             data-badge-motion={t.badgeMotion ?? 'land'}[39m
[31m+             aria-label={t.badgeLabel ?? `${t.badge} badge`}[39m
[31m+             aria-hidden={badgePhase === 'hidden'}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {t.badgeImageSrc ? ([39m
[31m+               <img[39m
[31m+                 className="ceremony-overlay__badge-image"[39m
[31m+                 src={t.badgeImageSrc}[39m
[31m+                 alt=""[39m
[31m+                 aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               t.badge[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         )[39m
[31m+       })}[39m
[31m+     </>[39m
[31m+   )[39m
[31m+ }[39m

}%0A+%0A+     // Badge animation timeline%0A+     addTimer(() => setBadgePhase('appearing'), APPEAR_DELAY)%0A+     addTimer(() => setBadgePhase('flying'), FLY_DELAY)%0A+     addTimer(() =>
